# events | raven client

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events.md)
.

### getRotations[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events#getrotations)

called when Minecraft gets your yaw and pitch for movement calculations, also applies for server-side rotations.

Copy

    float[] getRotations(float lastYaw, float lastPitch)

* * *

### onAntiCheatFlag[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events#onanticheatflag)

called when the AntiCheat module flags a player.

Copy

    void onAntiCheatFlag(String flag, Entity player)

* * *

### onBedWarsAlert[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events#onbedwarsalert)

called when the BedWars module sends an alert for a player.

Copy

    void onBedWarsAlert(String mode, Entity player)

* * *

### onChat[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events#onchat)

called whenever a message is sent in chat, return `false` to cancel.

Copy

    boolean onChat(String msg, int type)

* * *

### onDisable[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events#ondisable)

called whenever the script is disabled.

Copy

    void onDisable()

* * *

### onEnable[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events#onenable)

called whenever the script is enabled.

Copy

    void onEnable()

* * *

### onGuiUpdate[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events#onguiupdate)

called when a GUI updates.

Copy

    void onGuiUpdate(String name, boolean opened)

* * *

### onIRCMessage[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events#onircmessage)

called when you receive an IRC message, return `false` to cancel printing.

Copy

    boolean onIRCMessage(String message)

* * *

### onKey[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events#onkey)

called when a key is pressed.

Copy

    void onKey(String keyName, int keyCode, boolean state, boolean inGui)

* * *

### onLoad[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events#onload)

called when the script is loaded. register buttons/sliders here.

Copy

    void onLoad()

* * *

### onLocRaw[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events#onlocraw)

calls on `/locraw` (auto-runs on world join), gives raw response as string.

Copy

    void onLocRaw(String locraw)

* * *

### onMouse[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events#onmouse)

called whenever a button on the mouse is pressed, return `false` to cancel.

Copy

    boolean onMouse(int button, boolean state, int mouseX, int mouseY)

button

description

\-1

scroll wheel, state = up (true)/down (false)

0

left click

1

right click

2

middle click

* * *

### onPacketReceived[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events#onpacketreceived)

called before a packet gets processed by the client, return `false` to cancel.

Copy

    boolean onPacketReceived(SPacket packet)

* * *

### onPacketSent[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events#onpacketsent)

called before a packet gets sent to the server, return `false` to cancel.

Copy

    boolean onPacketSent(CPacket packet)

* * *

### onPostMotion[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events#onpostmotion)

called after updates sent to server.

Copy

    void onPostMotion()

* * *

### onPostPlayerInput[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events#onpostplayerinput)

called after the client processes player inputs.

Copy

    void onPostPlayerInput()

* * *

### onPreMotion[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events#onpremotion)

called before player updates are sent to the server.

see also: [`playerstate`](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/playerstate)
 for `PlayerState` properties.

Copy

    void onPreMotion(PlayerState state)

* * *

### onPreUpdate[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events#onpreupdate)

called at the beginning of every update to the local player.

Copy

    void onPreUpdate()

* * *

### onRenderTick[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events#onrendertick)

called every time the HUD renders.

Copy

    void onRenderTick(float partialTicks)

* * *

### onRenderWorld[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events#onrenderworld)

called every time the world renders.

Copy

    void onRenderWorld(float partialTicks)

* * *

### onWorldJoin[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events#onworldjoin)

called whenever an entity spawns in.

Copy

    void onWorldJoin(Entity entity)

# formatting | raven client

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/guide/formatting.md)
.

Raven scripts use **Java 8 syntax**, but they are not written like normal standalone Java files.

Do not include:

*   A `package` declaration
    
*   A `class` declaration
    
*   Any `import` statements
    

Raven handles the required structure automatically.

Scripts may only use the classes listed on the [`Imports`](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/imports)
 page, along with the available API wrappers. Additional imports are not supported. Fully qualified class names should not be used to bypass this restriction.

Nested `class`, `interface`, and `enum` declarations are supported.

Modifiers such as `public`, `private`, `protected`, and `static` are generally unnecessary, but normal Java access rules still apply. For example, a method implementing an interface method must be `public`.

#### Welcome to the Scripting API documentation.[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting#welcome-to-the-scripting-api-documentation)

Write simple Java scripts that hook into events, read game state, and automate actions.

Visit the [setup](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/guide/setup)
 page to get your environment ready and run your first script.

# setup | raven client

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/guide/setup.md)
.

### Installing the Java Development Kit[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/guide/setup#installing-the-java-development-kit)

You must install the Java Development Kit to create and use scripts!

A Java Runtime Environment (JRE) can run Java applications but does not include the tools required to compile Java scripts. To compile Java scripts you need a **Java Development Kit (JDK)**.

**Windows/Linux/macOS (Intel)**: Install the Adoptium Java 8 JDK [here](https://adoptium.net/en-GB/temurin/releases?version=8&os=any&arch=any)
.

**macOS (ARM)**: Install the Azul Zulu ARM64 Java 8 JDK [here](https://www.azul.com/downloads/?version=java-8-lts&os=macos&package=jdk#zulu)
.

* * *

### Setting the Java executable[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/guide/setup#setting-the-java-executable)

On Windows, the Java executable is typically located at either:

`C:\Program Files\Java\jdk` `<java-version>` `\bin\javaw.exe`

`C:\Program Files\Eclipse Adoptium\jdk` `<java-version>` `\bin\javaw.exe`

On Linux:

`/usr/lib/jvm/``<java-version>``/bin/java`

On macOS:

`/Library/Java/JavaVirtualMachines/``<java-version>``.jdk/Contents/Home/bin/java`

To configure the Java executable in the default Minecraft Launcher, follow these steps:

1

#### Open Installations[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/guide/setup#open-installations)

Navigate to the "Installations" tab in the launcher interface.

2

#### Select a profile[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/guide/setup#select-a-profile)

Locate the launch profile for which you want to set the Java executable.

3

#### Edit the profile[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/guide/setup#edit-the-profile)

On the right-hand side of the launch profile, click the button faced with three dots and select "Edit" from the drop-down menu.

4

#### Show more options[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/guide/setup#show-more-options)

Click on "More Options" to expand it and reveal additional settings.

5

#### Set Java executable[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/guide/setup#set-java-executable)

Locate the field labelled "Java executable" within the "More Options" section and paste the path of `javaw.exe` corresponding to your Java installation into the field.

* * *

### Creating Scripts[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/guide/setup#creating-scripts)

*   Raven scripts are coded in Java, which means your code must be in a `.java` file.
    
*   The recommended approach is to use a code editor, such as Brackets or Visual Studio Code.
    
*   All scripts must be placed in the `scripts` folder.
    
*   Script file names are invalid if they begin with an underscore, exceed 14 characters, conflict with an existing module name, or contain characters other than letters, numbers, underscores, and hyphens.
    

* * *

### Using Scripts[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/guide/setup#using-scripts)

*   The GUI has a category labelled **Scripts**. All scripts in your scripts folder will be displayed here.
    
*   To add scripts, click **Open Folder** within the Manager module and drag your scripts into the folder.
    
*   Whenever you introduce a new script or make modifications to an existing one, press the **Load Scripts** button to apply the changes.
    
*   If there is an error within a script, it will appear in red and cannot be enabled. An error message will be displayed in the chat window with details about the encountered issue.

# encryption | raven client

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/guide/encryption.md)
.

### Why Encryption?[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/guide/encryption#why-encryption)

By default, script source code can be easily accessed and altered. Encryption ensures your code remains secure and is only decrypted at runtime by the client.

To encrypt a script, use the **orchid bot** in the customer discord and run the command:

`/encrypt <script.java>`

Scripts cannot contain both plaintext and encrypted code.

Encryption is intended to discourage casual code theft, but it should not be considered a guarantee that your code cannot be analyzed or reverse engineered. Do not hardcode sensitive information such as API keys, tokens, passwords, or other confidential data into your scripts. Please note that attempting to reverse engineer encrypted scripts is prohibited under the [Raven Terms of Service](https://pastebin.com/raw/MFR7biX4)
.

A Java Runtime Environment (JRE) can run Java applications but does not include the tools required to compile Java scripts. To compile Java scripts you need a \*\*Java Development Kit (JDK)\*\*. {% endhint %} \*\*Windows/Linux/macOS (Intel)\*\*: Install the Adoptium Java 8 JDK \[here\](https://adoptium.net/en-GB/temurin/releases?version=8\\&os=any\\&arch=any). \*\*macOS (ARM)\*\*: Install the Azul Zulu ARM64 Java 8 JDK \[here\](https://www.azul.com/downloads/?version=java-8-lts\\&os=macos\\&package=jdk#zulu). \*\*\* ### Setting the Java executable {% hint style="info" %} On Windows, the Java executable is typically located at either: \`C:\\Program Files\\Java\\jdk\` \`\` \`\\bin\\javaw.exe\` \`C:\\Program Files\\Eclipse Adoptium\\jdk\` \`\` \`\\bin\\javaw.exe\` On Linux: \`/usr/lib/jvm/\`\`\`\`/bin/java\` On macOS: \`/Library/Java/JavaVirtualMachines/\`\`\`\`.jdk/Contents/Home/bin/java\` {% endhint %} To configure the Java executable in the default Minecraft Launcher, follow these steps: {% stepper %} {% step %} #### Open Installations Navigate to the "Installations" tab in the launcher interface. {% endstep %} {% step %} #### Select a profile Locate the launch profile for which you want to set the Java executable. {% endstep %} {% step %} #### Edit the profile On the right-hand side of the launch profile, click the button faced with three dots and select "Edit" from the drop-down menu. {% endstep %} {% step %} #### Show more options Click on "More Options" to expand it and reveal additional settings. {% endstep %} {% step %} #### Set Java executable Locate the field labelled "Java executable" within the "More Options" section and paste the path of \`javaw.exe\` corresponding to your Java installation into the field. {% endstep %} {% endstepper %} \*\*\* ### Creating Scripts \* Raven scripts are coded in Java, which means your code must be in a \`.java\` file. \* The recommended approach is to use a code editor, such as Brackets or Visual Studio Code. \* All scripts must be placed in the \`scripts\` folder. \* Script file names are invalid if they begin with an underscore, exceed 14 characters, conflict with an existing module name, or contain characters other than letters, numbers, underscores, and hyphens. \*\*\* ### Using Scripts \* The GUI has a category labelled \*\*Scripts\*\*. All scripts in your scripts folder will be displayed here. \* To add scripts, click \*\*Open Folder\*\* within the Manager module and drag your scripts into the folder. \* Whenever you introduce a new script or make modifications to an existing one, press the \*\*Load Scripts\*\* button to apply the changes. \* If there is an error within a script, it will appear in red and cannot be enabled. An error message will be displayed in the chat window with details about the encountered issue.

\> For the complete documentation index, see \[llms.txt\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/llms.txt). Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events.md). # events ### getRotations called when Minecraft gets your yaw and pitch for movement calculations, also applies for server-side rotations. \`\`\`java float\[\] getRotations(float lastYaw, float lastPitch) \`\`\` \*\*\* ### onAntiCheatFlag called when the AntiCheat module flags a player. \`\`\`java void onAntiCheatFlag(String flag, Entity player) \`\`\` \*\*\* ### onBedWarsAlert called when the BedWars module sends an alert for a player. \`\`\`java void onBedWarsAlert(String mode, Entity player) \`\`\` \*\*\* ### onChat called whenever a message is sent in chat, return \`false\` to cancel. \`\`\`java boolean onChat(String msg, int type) \`\`\` \*\*\* ### onDisable called whenever the script is disabled. \`\`\`java void onDisable() \`\`\` \*\*\* ### onEnable called whenever the script is enabled. \`\`\`java void onEnable() \`\`\` \*\*\* ### onGuiUpdate called when a GUI updates. \`\`\`java void onGuiUpdate(String name, boolean opened) \`\`\` \*\*\* ### onIRCMessage called when you receive an IRC message, return \`false\` to cancel printing. \`\`\`java boolean onIRCMessage(String message) \`\`\` \*\*\* ### onKey called when a key is pressed. \`\`\`java void onKey(String keyName, int keyCode, boolean state, boolean inGui) \`\`\` \*\*\* ### onLoad called when the script is loaded. register buttons/sliders here. \`\`\`java void onLoad() \`\`\` \*\*\* ### onLocRaw calls on \`/locraw\` (auto-runs on world join), gives raw response as string. \`\`\`java void onLocRaw(String locraw) \`\`\` \*\*\* ### onMouse called whenever a button on the mouse is pressed, return \`false\` to cancel. \`\`\`java boolean onMouse(int button, boolean state, int mouseX, int mouseY) \`\`\`

| button | description |
| --- | --- |
| \-1 | scroll wheel, state = up (true)/down (false) |
| 0   | left click |
| 1   | right click |
| 2   | middle click |

\*\*\* ### onPacketReceived called before a packet gets processed by the client, return \`false\` to cancel. \`\`\`java boolean onPacketReceived(SPacket packet) \`\`\` \*\*\* ### onPacketSent called before a packet gets sent to the server, return \`false\` to cancel. \`\`\`java boolean onPacketSent(CPacket packet) \`\`\` \*\*\* ### onPostMotion called after updates sent to server. \`\`\`java void onPostMotion() \`\`\` \*\*\* ### onPostPlayerInput called after the client processes player inputs. \`\`\`java void onPostPlayerInput() \`\`\` \*\*\* ### onPreMotion called before player updates are sent to the server. {% hint style="info" %} see also: \[\`playerstate\`\](/scripting/documentation/playerstate.md) for \`PlayerState\` properties. {% endhint %} \`\`\`java void onPreMotion(PlayerState state) \`\`\` \*\*\* ### onPreUpdate called at the beginning of every update to the local player. \`\`\`java void onPreUpdate() \`\`\` \*\*\* ### onRenderTick called every time the HUD renders. \`\`\`java void onRenderTick(float partialTicks) \`\`\` \*\*\* ### onRenderWorld called every time the world renders. \`\`\`java void onRenderWorld(float partialTicks) \`\`\` \*\*\* ### onWorldJoin called whenever an entity spawns in. \`\`\`java void onWorldJoin(Entity entity) \`\`\`

Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/readme.md). # main #### Welcome to the Scripting API documentation. Write simple Java scripts that hook into events, read game state, and automate actions. Visit the \[setup\](/scripting/guide/setup.md) page to get your environment ready and run your first script. \*\*\* ### Credits This documentation was created by \[blowsy\](https://www.youtube.com/@blowsy), \[pug\](https://www.youtube.com/@pugrilla), and \[desiyn\](https://www.youtube.com/@desIWNL).

# Unknown

\> For the complete documentation index, see \[llms.txt\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/llms.txt). Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/guide/encryption.md). # encryption ### Why Encryption? By default, script source code can be easily accessed and altered. Encryption ensures your code remains secure and is only decrypted at runtime by the client. To encrypt a script, use the \*\*orchid bot\*\* in the customer discord and run the command: \`/encrypt \` {% embed url="" %} {% hint style="warning" %} Scripts cannot contain both plaintext and encrypted code. {% endhint %} {% hint style="danger" %} Encryption is intended to discourage casual code theft, but it should not be considered a guarantee that your code cannot be analyzed or reverse engineered. Do not hardcode sensitive information such as API keys, tokens, passwords, or other confidential data into your scripts. Please note that attempting to reverse engineer encrypted scripts is prohibited under the \[Raven Terms of Service\](https://pastebin.com/raw/MFR7biX4). {% endhint %}

\[modules\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules.md): static class - \[network\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/network.md): static class - \[packets\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/packets.md): object class - \[playerstate\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/playerstate.md): object class - \[render\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/render.md): static class - \[request\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/request.md): object class - \[response\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/response.md): object class - \[tile entity\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/tile-entity.md): object class - \[util\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/util.md): static class - \[vec3\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/vec3.md): object class - \[world\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/world.md): static class - \[AutoClicker\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/combat/autoclicker.md): AutoClicker sends mouse clicks for you at a rate between Min CPS and Max CPS. - \[AimAssist\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/combat/aimassist.md): AimAssist helps keep your crosshair on a nearby entity while you aim. - \[AntiKnockback\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/combat/antiknockback.md): AntiKnockback changes the knockback Minecraft sends after an attack or explosion. - \[ClickAssist\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/combat/clickassist.md): ClickAssist adds extra clicks when you press a mouse button. - \[Hitbox\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/combat/hitbox.md): Hitbox expands the area Minecraft uses when it checks an entity for collision and attacks. - \[Hit Select\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/combat/hit-select.md): Hit Select delays selected attacks so they land at a better time. - \[Jump Reset\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/combat/jump-reset.md): Jump Reset jumps immediately after you receive knockback. - \[KillAura\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/combat/killaura.md): KillAura attacks nearby entities for you. - \[Reach\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/combat/reach.md): Reach changes how far away an entity can be when you attack it. - \[Reduce\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/combat/reduce.md): Reduce removes some of the movement slowdown that Minecraft applies after you hit an entity. - \[TPAura\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/combat/tpaura.md): TPAura moves you directly to a nearby player and attacks them. - \[Velocity\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/combat/velocity.md): Velocity controls how much knockback you receive after another entity hits you. - \[WTap\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/combat/wtap.md): WTap releases sprint after a hit and presses it again later. - \[AntiShuffle\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/render/antishuffle.md): Some servers use obfuscated text that flickers through different characters. - \[BedESP\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/render/bedesp.md): BedESP shows nearby beds through blocks. - \[BreakProgress\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/render/breakprogress.md): When you break a block, BreakProgress shows how much of the break is complete. - \[Chams\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/render/chams.md): Chams keeps player models visible through blocks. - \[ChestESP\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/render/chestesp.md): ChestESP marks nearby chests through blocks with a colored box. - \[Damage Tags\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/render/damage-tags.md): Damage Tags puts floating numbers beside entities when they take damage or gain health. - \[Extend Camera\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/render/extend-camera.md): In third-person view, Minecraft normally keeps the camera within 4 blocks of your character. - \[Fall View\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/render/fall-view.md): Fall View shows how far you have fallen and estimates what will happen when you land. - \[HUD\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/render/hud.md): The HUD shows which modules are active on screen. - \[Indicators\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/render/indicators.md): Indicators puts arrows around the center of your screen when projectiles are coming toward you. - \[ItemESP\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/render/itemesp.md): ItemESP marks dropped items on the ground. - \[MobESP\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/render/mobesp.md): MobESP shows mob entities through blocks with highlight boxes. - \[Nametags\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/render/nametags.md): Nametags replaces the normal floating player name with a display that can include health, armor, held-item details, and distance. - \[NoCameraClip\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/render/nocameraclip.md): In third-person view, Minecraft pulls the camera closer when a solid block is in the way. - \[NoHurtCam\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/render/nohurtcam.md): Taking damage normally makes the camera shake. - \[PlayerESP\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/render/playeresp.md): PlayerESP highlights players through blocks so you can keep track of their positions when the world blocks your view. - \[Radar\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/render/radar.md): Radar places nearby player positions on a small overlay. - \[Shaders\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/render/shaders.md): Shaders applies a full-screen post-processing effect after Minecraft draws the game view. - \[TargetHUD\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/render/targethud.md): TargetHUD shows a panel for the player currently selected by KillAura. - \[Tracers\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/render/tracers.md): Tracers draws lines from your position to nearby players. - \[Trajectories\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/render/trajectories.md): Trajectories draws a predicted flight path before you throw or shoot a supported projectile. - \[Xray\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/render/xray.md): Xray renders selected ores and blocks through terrain. - \[AntiAFK\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/player/antiafk.md): AntiAFK keeps your player active while you are away from the keyboard. - \[AntiFireball\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/player/antifireball.md): AntiFireball watches for incoming fireballs and performs the action needed to deflect them before they reach you. - \[AutoBlockin\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/player/autoblockin.md): AutoBlockin places blocks around you and near an enemy bed. - \[AutoJump\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/player/autojump.md): AutoJump watches the block ahead of you and jumps when your next step would leave the ledge. - \[AutoLadder\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/player/autoladder.md): AutoLadder places a ladder during a fall to slow or stop the impact. - \[AutoPlace\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/player/autoplace.md): AutoPlace keeps the right-click input held while you build. - \[AutoSwap\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/player/autoswap.md): AutoSwap changes to another block stack before the stack in your selected hotbar slot runs out. - \[AutoTool\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/player/autotool.md): AutoTool selects a suitable tool when you point at a block to mine. - \[BedAura\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/player/bedaura.md): BedAura targets and breaks enemy beds in Bed Wars. - \[Delay Remover\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/player/delay-remover.md): Delay Remover changes two old Minecraft timing limits. - \[FastMine\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/player/fastmine.md): FastMine changes how often the client sends block-break actions and how quickly blocks break. - \[FastPlace\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/player/fastplace.md): FastPlace reduces the delay between block placement attempts while you hold right-click. - \[Freecam\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/player/freecam.md): Freecam separates your camera from your player. - \[GhostHand\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/player/ghosthand.md): GhostHand lets you interact with a block when a player or mob is standing in the target space. - \[InvManager\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/player/invmanager.md): InvManager handles routine inventory work for you. - \[NoFall\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/player/nofall.md): NoFall changes the movement data sent while you are falling. - \[NoRotate\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/player/norotate.md): NoRotate prevents a server rotation update from turning your player. - \[SafeWalk\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/player/safewalk.md): SafeWalk keeps you from stepping off the block under your player. - \[Scaffold\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/player/scaffold.md): Scaffold places blocks below or beside you while you bridge across a gap. - \[Tower\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/player/tower.md): Tower places blocks below you while you jump upward, so you can build a vertical column without timing every placement yourself. - \[Water Bucket\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/player/water-bucket.md): Water Bucket places water during a fall to reduce the impact. - \[Bhop\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/movement/bhop.md): Bhop times your jumps as you land so you can keep moving without pressing jump for every hop. - \[Boost\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/movement/boost.md): Boost gives you a one-time burst of horizontal velocity. - \[Dolphin\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/movement/dolphin.md): Dolphin changes how quickly you move through water. - \[Fly\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/movement/fly.md): Fly changes the movement data sent to the server and tries to keep you in a flying state while you play in survival mode. - \[InvMove\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/movement/invmove.md): InvMove lets you use movement keys while an inventory or container screen is open. - \[KeepSprint\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/movement/keepsprint.md): KeepSprint changes how much sprint speed you lose after hitting an entity. - \[Long Jump\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/movement/long-jump.md): Long Jump adds forward or sideways movement to a jump. - \[NoSlow\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/movement/noslow.md): NoSlow changes the movement slowdown caused by use-item actions. - \[Speed\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/movement/speed.md): Speed increases horizontal movement without relying on jump timing. - \[Sprint\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/movement/sprint.md): Sprint activates sprinting automatically when the normal sprint conditions are met. - \[Stasis\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/movement/stasis.md): Stasis keeps your player in one position while it is enabled. - \[Stop Motion\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/movement/stop-motion.md): Stop Motion sets selected parts of your movement to zero when it runs. - \[TargetStrafe\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/movement/targetstrafe.md): TargetStrafe moves you around the target selected by KillAura while you attack. - \[Teleport\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/movement/teleport.md): Teleport moves you instantly to the block you are targeting. - \[Timer\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/movement/timer.md): Timer changes how quickly the client updates the game. - \[VClip\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/movement/vclip.md): VClip moves you vertically through blocks by sending position data to the server. - \[AutoRequeue\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/minigames/autorequeue.md): AutoRequeue queues another match in the same game mode after your current match ends. - \[AutoWho\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/minigames/autowho.md): AutoWho runs /who when a game starts so you can check the players in the match. - \[Bed Wars\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/minigames/bed-wars.md): Bed Wars watches the match for items and events that are easy to miss while you fight. - \[Bridge Info\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/minigames/bridge-info.md): Bridge Info adds a HUD for The Bridge. - \[Duels Stats\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/minigames/duels-stats.md): Duels Stats fetches Hypixel Duels statistics for players you meet in a Duels lobby. - \[Murder Mystery\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/minigames/murder-mystery.md): Murder Mystery helps you follow the murderer, detective, and other players during a round. - \[Sky Wars\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/minigames/sky-wars.md): Sky Wars adds a small overlay while you play. - \[Speed Builders\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/minigames/speed-builders.md): Speed Builders reads the round's template and tracks the blocks that remain. - \[Sumo Fences\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/minigames/sumo-fences.md): Sumo Fences draws a fence guide around the border of a Hypixel Sumo arena. - \[AntiCheat\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/other/anticheat.md): AntiCheat checks nearby players for configured cheat signatures. - \[Chat Bypass\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/other/chat-bypass.md): Chat Bypass replaces selected letters in your message with visually similar Unicode characters before the message is sent. - \[Fake Chat\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/other/fake-chat.md): When you need a reminder that only you can see, Fake Chat adds a local message to your chat window. - \[Hit Particles\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/other/hit-particles.md): Hit Particles adds a visual effect where your attack hits. - \[Latency Alerts\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/other/latency-alerts.md): Latency Alerts watches the delay between your client and the server. - \[Name Hider\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/other/name-hider.md): Name Hider replaces your username with a name you choose while the module is active. - \[Spammer\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/other/spammer.md): Spammer repeats one configured message at a fixed interval. - \[Backtrack\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/network/backtrack.md): When a player moves just outside your crosshair, Backtrack can keep a recent position available for an attack. - \[Blink\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/network/blink.md): Blink holds your outbound movement packets before they reach the server. - \[Fake Lag\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/network/fake-lag.md): Fake Lag delays packets sent from your client to the server. - \[Freeze\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/network/freeze.md): Freeze holds packets coming from the server. - \[Lag Range\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/network/lag-range.md): Lag Range delays position updates from nearby players based on their distance. - \[Limbo\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/network/limbo.md): If you need to leave a Hypixel game without closing Minecraft, Limbo sends you to Hypixel Limbo. - \[VPN Status\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/network/vpn-status.md): VPN Status checks whether your connection uses a virtual private network and reports the result in chat. - \[Assistant\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/client/assistant.md): Use Assistant when you want to ask a question without leaving Minecraft. - \[Command Line\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/client/command-line.md): Command Line opens an overlay where you can enter Raven commands without opening the chat box. - \[Chat Commands\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/client/chat-commands.md): Chat Commands lets Raven handle commands that you enter in normal Minecraft chat. - \[IRC\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/client/irc.md): IRC adds a shared chat channel for Raven B4 users. - \[GUI\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/client/gui.md): GUI controls the Raven module menu. - \[Settings\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/client/settings.md): The Settings module controls values shared by Raven and its modules. - \[Extra Bobbing\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/fun/extra-bobbing.md): If walking feels too still, Extra Bobbing makes the hand and camera movement more noticeable. - \[Flame Trail\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/fun/flame-trail.md): Flame Trail leaves flame particles behind your player while you walk. - \[SlyPort\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/fun/slyport.md): SlyPort chooses a nearby target, teleports you behind it, and attacks immediately. - \[Spin\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/fun/spin.md): Spin rotates your player model for a cosmetic effect. - \[AntiBot\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/world/antibot.md): AntiBot checks player-like entities before Raven's targeting and display modules use them. - \[Weather\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/world/weather.md): Weather changes the time and weather you see without changing the server world.

---

# Unknown

\> For the complete documentation index, see \[llms.txt\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/llms.txt). Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/guide/formatting.md). # formatting Raven scripts use \*\*Java 8 syntax\*\*, but they are not written like normal standalone Java files. Do not include: \* A \`package\` declaration \* A \`class\` declaration \* Any \`import\` statements Raven handles the required structure automatically. Scripts may only use the classes listed on the \[\`Imports\`\](/scripting/documentation/imports.md) page, along with the available API wrappers. Additional imports are not supported. Fully qualified class names should not be used to bypass this restriction. Nested \`class\`, \`interface\`, and \`enum\` declarations are supported. Modifiers such as \`public\`, \`private\`, \`protected\`, and \`static\` are generally unnecessary, but normal Java access rules still apply. For example, a method implementing an interface method must be \`public\`.

# json | raven client

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/json.md)
.

Json[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/json#json)

----------------------------------------------------------------------

`Json` represents parsed json data. see sections below for available helpers.

### constructor[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/json#constructor)

creates a new json object from a string.

Copy

    Json json = new Json(String contents);

* * *

### object[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/json#object)

returns the json object itself or a named member as a new `Json` instance.

Copy

    Json obj = json.object();
    Json member = json.object(String memberName);

* * *

### array[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/json#array)

returns the json array or a named member as a list of `Json` objects.

Copy

    List<Json> array = json.array();
    List<Json> memberArray = json.array(String memberName);

* * *

### map[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/json#map)

returns a map of object members to `Json` values.

* * *

### get[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/json#get)

returns the string value of a member, optionally providing a default.

* * *

### exists[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/json#exists)

returns `true` if the json value is not null.

* * *

### equals[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/json#equals)

compares two json values for equality.

* * *

### string[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/json#string)

returns the json value as a string. non-primitive values are serialized.

### click[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/inventory#click)

### getBookContents[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/inventory#getbookcontents)

returns the pages of the currently open book as raw json strings.

Copy

    List<String> pages = inventory.getBookContents();

### getChest[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/inventory#getchest)

returns the chest name when a chest is open, otherwise an empty string.

### getChestSize[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/inventory#getchestsize)

returns the size of the opened chest inventory, or `0` if no chest is open.

Copy

    int chestSize = inventory.getChestSize();

* * *

### getContainer[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/inventory#getcontainer)

returns the simple class name of the currently open container.

* * *

### getSize[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/inventory#getsize)

returns the size of the player's inventory.

* * *

returns the currently selected hotbar slot index.

* * *

, or `null` when unavailable.

### getStackInSlot[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/inventory#getstackinslot)

returns the item in the specified player inventory slot as an [`ItemStack`](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/itemstack)
.

* * *

### open[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/inventory#open)

simulates opening the inventory gui.

* * *

### setSlot[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/inventory#setslot)

sets the currently selected hotbar slot.

# load string | raven client

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/loadstring.md)
.

Must be on a separate line in the following format:

Copy

    load - "URL"

Loaded content will replace the entire script source code.

Nested load strings are not supported.

* * *

### Example[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/loadstring#example)

Here is an example script hosted on [Pastebin](https://pastebin.com/93hWSJeN)
:

Copy

    void onEnable() {
        client.print("enabled script!");
    }

You can load it using the Pastebin raw link:

Copy

    load - "https://pastebin.com/raw/93hWSJeN"

Alternatively, load from a local directory:

Copy

    load - "file:///C:/Users/user/Desktop/script.txt"

[Previouskeybinds](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/keybinds)
[Nextmodules](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules)

Last updated 15 days ago

.

minecraft keycodes: [https://minecraft.fandom.com/wiki/Key\_codes](https://minecraft.fandom.com/wiki/Key_codes)

### getKeycode[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/keybinds#getkeycode)

returns the keycode for the specified minecraft keybind name.

Copy

    int code = keybinds.getKeycode(String key);

### getMousePosition[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/keybinds#getmouseposition)

returns the current mouse position in pixels as `[x, y]`.

### isKeyDown[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/keybinds#iskeydown)

returns `true` if the specified keycode is held.

Copy

### isMouseDown[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/keybinds#ismousedown)

returns `true` if the specified mouse button is pressed. use `0` for left, `1` for right, and `2` for middle click.

Copy

    boolean mouseDown = keybinds.isMouseDown(int button);

* * *

### isPressed[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/keybinds#ispressed)

returns `true` if the named keybind is currently pressed.

* * *

### leftClick[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/keybinds#leftclick)

simulates a left mouse click.

* * *

### rightClick[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/keybinds#rightclick)

simulates a right mouse click.

* * *

### setPressed[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/keybinds#setpressed)

sets the pressed state of a keybind as defined in `options.txt`.

# gl | raven client

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl.md)
.

advanced rendering api - improper use may cause visual glitches. use [`render`](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/render)
 methods when possible.

### alpha[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#alpha)

enables or disables alpha testing.

Copy

    gl.alpha(boolean enabled);

* * *

### begin[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#begin)

starts specifying vertices for a primitive shape.

Copy

    gl.begin(int mode);

* * *

### blend[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#blend)

enables or disables blending.

Copy

    gl.blend(boolean enabled);

* * *

### color[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#color)

sets the current drawing color.

Copy

    gl.color(int r, int g, int b, int a);
    gl.color(double r, double g, double b, double a);
    gl.color(float r, float g, float b, float a);
    gl.color(int rgb);
    gl.resetColor(); // set to white

* * *

### cull[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#cull)

enables or disables face culling.

* * *

### depth[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#depth)

enables or disables depth testing.

* * *

### depthMask[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#depthmask)

sets whether writing to the depth buffer is enabled.

* * *

### disable[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#disable)

disables an opengl capability.

* * *

### disableItemLighting[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#disableitemlighting)

disables standard item lighting.

* * *

### enable[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#enable)

enables an opengl capability.

* * *

### enableItemLighting[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#enableitemlighting)

enables standard item lighting. set `gui` to `true` when rendering in guis.

* * *

### end[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#end)

completes the current vertex specification.

* * *

### lighting[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#lighting)

enables or disables lighting.

* * *

### lineSmooth[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#linesmooth)

enables or disables line smoothing.

* * *

### lineWidth[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#linewidth)

sets the width of lines.

* * *

### normal[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#normal)

specifies a normal vector for lighting calculations.

* * *

### pop[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#pop)

pops the current matrix stack.

* * *

### push[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#push)

pushes the current matrix stack.

* * *

### rotate[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#rotate)

applies rotation around the specified axis.

* * *

### scale[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#scale)

scales the current transformation matrix.

* * *

### scissor (box)[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#scissor-box)

sets the scissor rectangle.

* * *

### scissor (toggle)[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#scissor-toggle)

enables or disables scissoring.

* * *

### texture2d[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#texture2d)

enables or disables 2d texturing.

* * *

### translate[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#translate)

translates the current transformation matrix.

* * *

### vertex2[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#vertex2)

specifies a 2d vertex.

* * *

### vertex3[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/gl#vertex3)

specifies a 3d vertex.

# network | raven client

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/network.md)
.

assumes variable `networkPlayer` is defined.

example: `NetworkPlayer networkPlayer = entity.getNetworkPlayer();`

see also: [`entity.getNetworkPlayer()`](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getnetworkplayer)
 or [`world.getNetworkPlayers()`](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/world#getnetworkplayers)

### getCape[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/network#getcape)

returns the cape resource path for the player, or `null` when unavailable.

Copy

    String cape = networkPlayer.getCape();

* * *

### getDisplayName[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/network#getdisplayname)

returns the formatted display name of the player.

Copy

    String displayName = networkPlayer.getDisplayName();

* * *

### getName[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/network#getname)

returns the unformatted player name.

Copy

    String name = networkPlayer.getName();

* * *

### getPing[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/network#getping)

returns the ping of the player in milliseconds.

* * *

### getSkinData[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/network#getskindata)

returns the skin data encoded as a json string, or `null` if missing.

* * *

### getUUID[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/network#getuuid)

returns the uuid of the player.

[Previousmodules](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules)
[Nextpackets](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/packets)

# playerstate | raven client

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/playerstate.md)
.

PlayerState[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/playerstate#playerstate)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

`PlayerState` represents a player's position, rotation, and movement state.

property

type

description

x

`double`

x coordinate

y

`double`

y coordinate

z

`double`

z coordinate

yaw

`float`

yaw rotation

pitch

`float`

pitch rotation

onGround

`boolean`

whether the player is on ground

isSprinting

`boolean`

whether the player is sprinting

isSneaking

`boolean`

whether the player is sneaking

### constructor[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/playerstate#constructor)

creates a new player state with individual parameters.

Copy

    PlayerState state = new PlayerState(double x, double y, double z, float yaw, float pitch, boolean onGround, boolean sprinting, boolean sneaking);

* * *

### constructor[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/playerstate#constructor-1)

creates a new player state from an array of data.

Copy

    PlayerState state = new PlayerState(Object[] data);

* * *

### asArray[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/playerstate#asarray)

returns the player state data as an object array.

# entity | raven client

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity.md)
.

assumes variable `entity` is defined.

example: `Entity entity = client.getPlayer();`

Entity[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#entity)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------

`Entity` represents an entity in the world with its properties and data.

property

type

description

type

`String`

simple class name of the entity

entityId

`int`

entity id in the world

isLiving

`boolean`

`true` if the entity extends `EntityLivingBase`

isPlayer

`boolean`

`true` if the entity is a player

isUser

`boolean`

`true` if the entity is the local player

* * *

### distanceToGround[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#distancetoground)

returns the distance from the entity to the ground.

Copy

    double distance = entity.distanceToGround();

* * *

### getAbsorption[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getabsorption)

returns the absorption amount, or `-1` for non-living entities.

Copy

    float absorption = entity.getAbsorption();

* * *

### getArmorInSlot[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getarmorinslot)

returns the armor item in the specified slot as an [`ItemStack`](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/itemstack)
. valid slots are `0-3`.

* * *

### getBlockPosition[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getblockposition)

returns the entity's block position as a [`Vec3`](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/vec3)
.

* * *

### getBPS[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getbps)

returns the entity's speed in blocks per second.

* * *

### getDisplayName[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getdisplayname)

returns the display name of the entity.

* * *

### getEyeHeight[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#geteyeheight)

returns the eye height.

* * *

### getFallDistance[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getfalldistance)

returns the fall distance.

* * *

### getFacing[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getfacing)

returns the horizontal facing direction.

* * *

### getHealth[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#gethealth)

returns the current health, or `-1` for non-living entities.

* * *

### getHeldItem[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#gethelditem)

returns the held item as an [`ItemStack`](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/itemstack)
, or `null` when empty.

* * *

### getHeight[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getheight)

returns the entity height.

* * *

### getHurtTime[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#gethurttime)

returns the hurt time, or `-1` for non-living entities.

* * *

### getLastPosition[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getlastposition)

returns the previous tick position as a [`Vec3`](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/vec3)
.

* * *

### getMaxHealth[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getmaxhealth)

returns the maximum health, or `-1` for non-living entities.

* * *

### getMaxHurtTime[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getmaxhurttime)

returns the maximum hurt time, or `-1` for non-living entities.

* * *

### getMoveAttribute[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getmoveattribute)

returns current movement speed attribute `getAIMoveSpeed`, or `-1` for non-player entities.

* * *

### getName[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getname)

returns the registry or display name of the entity.

* * *

### getNetworkPlayer[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getnetworkplayer)

returns the [`NetworkPlayer`](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/network)
 wrapper for this entity.

* * *

### getPitch[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getpitch)

returns the entity pitch.

* * *

### getPosition[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getposition)

returns the current position as a [`Vec3`](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/vec3)
.

* * *

### getPotionEffects[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getpotioneffects)

returns active potion effects as a list of `[id, name, amplifier, duration]`, or `null` when none.

* * *

### getPrevPitch[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getprevpitch)

returns the entity's previous pitch.

* * *

### getPrevYaw[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getprevyaw)

returns the entity's previous yaw.

* * *

### getSpeed[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getspeed)

returns the horizontal movement speed per tick.

* * *

### getStackSize[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getstacksize)

returns the stack size, or `-1` if not of type `EntityItem`.

* * *

### getSwingProgress[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getswingprogress)

returns the swing progress tick count, or `-1` for non-living entities.

* * *

### getTicksExisted[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getticksexisted)

returns the ticks existed value.

* * *

### getUUID[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getuuid)

returns the entity uuid as a string.

* * *

### getWidth[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getwidth)

returns the entity width.

* * *

### getYaw[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#getyaw)

returns the entity yaw.

* * *

### isBurning[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#isburning)

returns `true` if the entity is burning.

* * *

### isCollided[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#iscollided)

returns `true` if the entity is colliding.

* * *

### isCollidedHorizontally[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#iscollidedhorizontally)

returns `true` if the entity collides horizontally.

* * *

### isCollidedVertically[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#iscollidedvertically)

returns `true` if the entity collides vertically.

* * *

### isConsuming[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#isconsuming)

returns `true` if the player entity is consuming an item.

* * *

### isDead[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#isdead)

returns `true` if the entity is dead.

* * *

### isHoldingBlock[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#isholdingblock)

returns `true` if the entity is holding a block item.

* * *

### isHoldingSword[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#isholdingsword)

returns `true` if the entity is holding a sword.

* * *

### isHoldingWeapon[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#isholdingweapon)

returns `true` if the entity is holding a weapon recognised by raven.

* * *

### isInvisible[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#isinvisible)

returns `true` if the entity is invisible.

* * *

### isInLava[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#isinlava)

returns `true` if the entity is in lava.

* * *

### isInLiquid[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#isinliquid)

returns `true` if the entity is in water or lava.

* * *

### isInWater[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#isinwater)

returns `true` if the entity is in water.

* * *

### isOnEdge[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#isonedge)

returns `true` if the entity is standing on an edge.

* * *

### onGround[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#onground)

returns `true` if the entity is on the ground.

* * *

### isOnLadder[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#isonladder)

returns `true` if the entity is on a ladder.

* * *

### isRiding[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#isriding)

returns `true` if the entity is riding another entity.

* * *

### isSleeping[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#issleeping)

returns `true` if the player entity is sleeping.

* * *

### isSneaking[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#issneaking)

returns `true` if the entity is sneaking.

* * *

### isSprinting[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#issprinting)

returns `true` if the entity is sprinting.

* * *

### isSwinging[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#isswinging)

returns `true` if the entity is currently swinging.

* * *

### isUsingItem[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#isusingitem)

returns `true` if the player entity is using an item.

* * *

### setPitch[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#setpitch)

sets the entity pitch.

* * *

### setPosition[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#setposition)

sets the entity position.

* * *

### setPrevPitch[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#setprevpitch)

sets the entity's previous pitch.

* * *

### setPrevYaw[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#setprevyaw)

sets the entity's previous yaw.

* * *

### setYaw[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity#setyaw)

sets the entity yaw.

# request | raven client

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/request.md)
.

Request[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/request#request)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------

`Request` represents an http request with methods, headers, and content.

### constructor[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/request#constructor)

creates a new request to a specified url. supported methods: `GET`, `POST`.

Copy

    Request request = new Request(String method, String url);

* * *

### addHeader[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/request#addheader)

adds a header to the request.

Copy

    request.addHeader(String header, String value);

* * *

### setUserAgent[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/request#setuseragent)

sets the user-agent header for the request.

Copy

    request.setUserAgent(String userAgent);

* * *

### setConnectTimeout[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/request#setconnecttimeout)

sets the connection timeout in milliseconds.

* * *

### setReadTimeout[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/request#setreadtimeout)

sets the read timeout in milliseconds.

* * *

### setContent[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/request#setcontent)

sets the content to be sent with a post request.

* * *

### fetch[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/request#fetch)

executes the request and returns a [`Response`](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/response)
.

Response[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/response#response)

`Response` represents the result of an http request with status code and content.

* * *

returns the response body parsed as a Json object.

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules.md)
.

### disable[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#disable)

disables the specified module.

Copy

    modules.disable(String moduleName);

* * *

### enable[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#enable)

enables the specified module.

Copy

    modules.enable(String moduleName);

* * *

### getAimAssistTarget[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#getaimassisttarget)

returns the current AimAssist target as an [`Entity`](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity)
 or `null` if none.

Copy

    Entity target = modules.getAimAssistTarget();

* * *

### getBacktrackTarget[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#getbacktracktarget)

returns the current Backtrack target as an [`Entity`](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity)
 or `null` if none.

Copy

    Entity target = modules.getBacktrackTarget();

* * *

### getBedAuraPosition[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#getbedauraposition)

returns the BedAura target position as a [`Vec3`](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/vec3)
, or `null` when inactive.

* * *

### getBedAuraProgress[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#getbedauraprogress)

returns the current break progress of the targeted bed as `[modified, vanilla]`.

* * *

### getButton[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#getbutton)

returns the value of a registered button setting.

* * *

### getCategories[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#getcategories)

returns all module categories mapped to their module names.

* * *

### getKillAuraTarget[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#getkillauratarget)

returns the current KillAura target as an [`Entity`](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity)
 or `null` if none.

* * *

### getLagRangeTarget[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#getlagrangetarget)

returns the current Lag Range target as an [`Entity`](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/entity)
 or `null` if none.

* * *

### getSettings[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#getsettings)

returns every setting registered by the specified module.

* * *

### getWhitelistedBedPos[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#getwhitelistedbedpos)

returns the whitelisted bed position as a [`Vec3`](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/vec3)
, or `null` if none. persists across worlds.

* * *

### getSlider[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#getslider)

returns the current value of a slider setting. string-array sliders return the selected index.

* * *

### getSliderString[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#getsliderstring)

returns the current string value of a slider setting. string-array sliders return the selected mode name.

* * *

### getSliderMin[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#getslidermin)

returns the minimum value of a slider setting.

* * *

### getSliderMax[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#getslidermax)

returns the maximum value of a slider setting.

* * *

### getSliderInterval[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#getsliderinterval)

returns the interval of a slider setting.

* * *

### isEnabled[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#isenabled)

returns `true` if the specified module is enabled.

* * *

### isHidden[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#ishidden)

returns `true` if the specified module is hidden.

* * *

### isScaffolding[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#isscaffolding)

returns `true` when the Scaffold module is currently active.

* * *

### isTowering[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#istowering)

returns `true` when the Tower module is currently active.

* * *

### registerButton[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#registerbutton)

registers a toggleable button with its default value, only works [`onLoad`](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events#onload)
.

* * *

### registerDescription[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#registerdescription)

registers a description displayed in the script options, only works [`onLoad`](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events#onload)
.

* * *

### registerSlider[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#registerslider)

registers a slider. provide numeric bounds or an array of options, only works [`onLoad`](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/events#onload)
.

* * *

### setButton[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#setbutton)

updates the value of an existing button setting.

* * *

### setSlider[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/documentation/modules#setslider)

updates the value of a slider setting.

# Backtrack | raven client

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/network/backtrack.md)
.

It buffers position updates for nearby players, so an attack can use a position the target has already left. It changes packet timing, not attack range. That makes it a lag based alternative when Reach is not usable. With a larger delay, the target can look frozen or leave a visible ghost.

Keep the buffer small[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/network/backtrack#keep-the-buffer-small)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Delay[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/network/backtrack#delay)

`Delay` decides how far back Backtrack can look. The slider accepts `50-1500 ms`. Start near `50-150 ms` if you want the target to stay close to its current position. Higher values make the gap easier to see.

### Distance (min) and Distance (max)[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/network/backtrack#distance-min-and-distance-max)

These settings limit when Backtrack buffers a target. `Distance (min)` keeps the module from buffering at very close range. `Distance (max)` stops it from tracking targets that are too far away. A `2-5 block` window is a useful starting point.

Choose when it can track[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/network/backtrack#choose-when-it-can-track)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

`Cooldown` pauses buffering after you land a hit. Set it to `0` to disable the pause. The slider accepts `0-8 seconds`.

`FOV` limits buffering to targets in the selected angle in front of you. `Only hittable` skips positions that you could not hit because of terrain or distance. `Only movement` delays movement updates without delaying other entity updates. Leave it off when you want the full position buffer.

`Weapon only` limits Backtrack to weapon-held situations. `Ignore teammates` skips teammates. `Disable adventure` turns the module off in Adventure mode.

**Legit setup**

Start with `Delay` near `50 ms`, a `2-5 block` distance window, `Cooldown` disabled, and `Only movement` disabled. Keep `Flush on damage` enabled so the buffer clears when you take a hit.

Clear and inspect the buffer[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/network/backtrack#clear-and-inspect-the-buffer)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

`Flush on damage` clears stored positions when you take damage. This prevents Backtrack from keeping an old target position after the fight moves on.

`Show server position` draws a ghost at each player's real server position. Use it to see the distance between the position on your screen and the position used by the server.

If you want to delay player positions by distance instead, see [Lag Range](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/network/lag-range)
. If you want to delay your own outgoing movement, see [Fake Lag](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/network/fake-lag)
.

[PreviousSpammer](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/other/spammer)
[NextBlink](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/network/blink)

# AntiBot | raven client

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/world/antibot.md)
.

It can filter NPCs, Watchdog bots, and other entities that are not real players.

Decide when an entity is a bot[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/world/antibot#decide-when-an-entity-is-a-bot)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

`Delay` controls how long Raven waits before it classifies a new entity as a bot. This gives a real player time to finish joining. A value of `0` disables the delay. The value can be set from `0` to `10` seconds.

`Pit spawn` is for Hypixel Pit bot detection. Raven filters bots that spawn below the selected Y-level, the vertical world coordinate. Its value can be set from `79` to `140`; `79` disables this check.

Leave `Pit spawn` at `79` outside Hypixel Pit. Use another value only for the Pit check.

Use server checks[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/world/antibot#use-server-checks)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Enable `Filter watchdog` to exclude Watchdog-flagged entities from targeting. Enable `Tab list` to use the server tab list as a player check. Entities that are not on the tab list are treated as bots.

Keep `Tab list` enabled when you want Raven to ignore entities that are not listed by the server.

Show world changes[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/world/antibot#show-world-changes)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Enable `Print world join` to print a chat message when you enter a new world.

Related modules[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/world/antibot#related-modules)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   [KillAura](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/combat/killaura)
     targets nearby entities automatically.
    
*   [PlayerESP](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/render/playeresp)
     highlights players on screen.
    

[PreviousSpin](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/fun/spin)
[NextWeather](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/world/weather)

# Settings | raven client

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/client/settings.md)
.

Use it for client cosmetics, HUD display, combat targeting, profiles, and theme colors.

Change your local appearance[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/client/settings#change-your-local-appearance)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

`Custom cape` changes the cape Raven renders for you. Other players do not see it. The choices are `None`, `Purple`, `Red`, `Green`, `Yellow`, `Aqua`, `Anime`, and `Jewstein`.

`Default theme` chooses the colors for modules whose own theme is set to `Default`. Available themes are `Rainbow`, `Aurora`, `Cherry`, `Cotton candy`, `Flare`, `Flower`, `Forest`, `Frost`, `Gold`, `Grayscale`, `Inferno`, `Royal`, `Sandstorm`, `Sky`, and `Vine`.

Change HUD and chat display[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/client/settings#change-hud-and-chat-display)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Use `Add brackets to distance` to wrap distance values in nametags and ESP displays. `Show health as hearts` replaces health numbers with hearts, while `Show heart symbol` adds `♥` next to health values.

`Info HUD background` chooses `None`, `Rect`, or `Round` for info HUD backgrounds. Use `Edit position` to move the info HUD on your screen.

You can enable `Middle click friends` to add a player to your friends list with a middle click. `Set chat as inventory` makes Raven treat the chat screen as an inventory screen.

`Hide first person self ESP` hides your own ESP box in first-person view when a lag module draws one.

Aim and move with combat modules[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/client/settings#aim-and-move-with-combat-modules)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

`Aim closest point` makes aim rotations target the nearest point on a target hitbox instead of its center. `Rotate body` turns your body model to match those rotations. `Full body` applies the rotation to your whole body instead of only your head.

Enable `Movement fix` to correct your movement direction when aim rotations change your view. `Jitter yaw` adds random left-to-right rotation, with a value from `0` to `50`.

Keep `Movement fix` enabled if you want movement to stay aligned with aim rotations.

`Weapon list` tells combat modules which item types count as weapons. Each item has its own toggle: `Axe`, `Enchanted` for any enchanted item, `Fist` for an empty hand, `Hoe`, `Rod`, `Shovel`, and `Stick`.

Load and announce profiles[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/client/settings#load-and-announce-profiles)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

`Disable modules on load` turns off all modules when you load a profile. `Send message on enable` sends a chat message when a profile is enabled.

Adjust theme animation[](https://daikirai.gitbook.io/scripting/client/settings#adjust-theme-animation)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

`Offset` changes the color offset used by theme colors. Its value can be set from `-4` to `4`. `Time multiplier` changes the speed of theme color animations, from `0.1` to `4`.
